| test/unit/instance.test.js | use class objects > allow new Hooks() | instance.test.mjs :: Marked > use class objects > allow new Hooks() | Exercised through the public Marked, hooks, renderer, tokenizer, and options API. The assertion is the value a caller receives. |
| test/unit/bin.test.js | bin/marked > string > -s | cli.test.mjs :: -s renders a heading from the argument | The -s flag compiles that markdown string and prints the HTML. |
| test/unit/bin.test.js | bin/marked > string > --string | cli.test.mjs :: --string renders a heading from the argument | The --string flag compiles that markdown string and prints the HTML. |
| test/unit/bin.test.js | bin/marked > config > -c | cli.test.mjs :: -c loads a config module that turns on breaks | A config module enabling breaks joins the two lines with a break element. |
| test/unit/bin.test.js | bin/marked > config > --config | cli.test.mjs :: --config loads a config module that turns on breaks | A config module enabling breaks joins the two lines with a break element. |
| test/unit/bin.test.js | bin/marked > config > config path with hash | cli.test.mjs :: --config loads a module whose filename contains a hash | A # character in the config filename still loads the module. |
| test/unit/bin.test.js | bin/marked > config > config not found | cli.test.mjs :: missing --config exits with a failure status and no html | A missing config path fails the process. Stderr wording is not asserted. The matching success case is the config that enables breaks. |
| test/unit/bin.test.js | bin/marked > input > input file positional | cli.test.mjs :: a positional file is compiled | The last positional path is read as markdown and compiled. |
| test/unit/bin.test.js | bin/marked > input > input file not found | cli.test.mjs :: a missing positional file exits with a failure status and no html | A missing input path fails the process and writes no HTML. Paired with the positional file that does compile. |
| test/unit/bin.test.js | bin/marked > input > input file not found --input | cli.test.mjs :: a missing --input file exits with a failure status and no html | A missing --input path fails the process and writes no HTML. Paired with --input of a real file. |
| test/unit/bin.test.js | bin/marked > input > reads from stdin when no files are provided | cli.test.mjs :: stdin is compiled when no path is given | With no file arguments, standard input is compiled. |
| test/unit/bin.test.js | exec > stdin | cli.test.mjs :: stdin is compiled when no path is given | Same stdin behavior, reached through the real executable. |
| test/unit/bin.test.js | exec > string | cli.test.mjs :: -s renders a heading from the argument | Same -s behavior, reached through the real executable. |
| test/unit/bin.test.js | exec > input | cli.test.mjs :: --input compiles a file | The --input path is read as markdown and compiled. |
| test/unit/bin.test.js | exec > input file positional | cli.test.mjs :: a positional file is compiled | Same positional-file behavior, reached through the real executable. |
| test/unit/bin.test.js | exec > input last file positional | cli.test.mjs :: the last positional file is the one compiled | When several positional paths are given, the last one is compiled. An earlier missing path does not replace it. |
| test/cjs-test.cjs | require() then marked.parse('# test') includes an h1 | module.test.mjs :: parse turns a heading into an h1 and plain text into a paragraph | The CJS loader is not the provided module interface. The behavior the file checks is that parsing a heading emits an h1, which is tested through APP_MODULE, with a plain paragraph as the paired case. |
| test/umd-test.js | UMD global marked.parse('# test') includes an h1 | module.test.mjs :: parse turns a heading into an h1 and plain text into a paragraph | The UMD global binding is not APP_MODULE or APP_CMD. The behavior the file checks is that parsing a heading emits an h1, tested through APP_MODULE, with a plain paragraph as the paired case. |
| test/types/marked.ts | tsc project typecheck | not observable | Compile-time types only. Nothing a caller can observe at runtime is asserted. |
| test/bench.js | benchmark script | not observable | Prints timings and has no pass/fail behavior a client can observe. |
| test/rules.js | regex source dump | not observable | Prints internal regular-expression sources. Parsing behavior those rules implement is covered by the spec and lexer HTML checks. |
| test/recheck.ts | static ReDoS scan of rule sources | not observable | Analyzes internal regular expressions. The runtime bound on the published patterns is covered by the ReDOS spec cases, which must finish within 1 second. |
| test/update-specs.js | spec fixture updater | excluded | Maintainer script that rewrites fixtures. It is not a behavioral test. |
